Regular constraints, by contrast, describe finite-horizon control requirements by an automaton: fixed positions, forced endings, metrical patterns, and forbidden copied fragments are all special cases. Existing exact methods already handle regular constraints with belief propagation for first-order Markov chains. The contribution here is the variable-order extension: identifying the state space on which the existing BP-regular machinery must be run when the generator is a variable-order/backoff model. A first-order constraint layer can enforce useful support conditions, but it computes future mass after merging histories that a variable-order generator deliberately keeps distinct. We formalize this mismatch and give the sparse construction obtained by replacing the first-order Markov state with the observed context state, then taking the standard product with the regular constraint automaton. For a fixed trained context graph and automaton, inference is linear in the sequence horizon; in general it is polynomial in the number of reachable product edges. This gives the correct variable-order distribution conditioned on regular constraints without expanding to all K-tuples. The same finite-source interface supports reversible data augmentation by inverse count lookup, matching materialized transposition augmentation without storing transformed corpora. We also separate exact BP inference from generation-time backoff policies, such as singleton avoidance, whose stochastic semantics must be made explicit if exactness is claimed.

</details>


### [255] [AgentEscapeBench: Evaluating Out-of-Domain Tool-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07926)
*Zhengkang Guo,Yiyang Li,Lin Qiu,Xiaohua Wang,Jingwen Xv,Dongyu Ru,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoqing Zheng,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentEscapeBench是一个基于密室逃脱模式的基准测试，用于评估LLM驱动智能体在面对深层依赖约束下进行工具推理的能力。实验表明，随着依赖深度增加，智能体性能显著下降，人类从98.3%降至80.0%，最佳模型从90.0%降至60.0%，主要失败原因在于长期状态跟踪、线索遵循和中间结果传播的断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估多局限于熟悉的工作流程和短程交互，缺乏对其在深层工具依赖约束下进行持续性推理能力的系统评估。

Method: 设计了AgentEscapeBench基准，包含270个实例、五个难度层级，每个任务定义了一个有向无环依赖图，要求智能体调用真实外部函数、追踪渐进揭示的隐藏状态、传播中间结果并提交可验证的最终答案。

Result: 对16个LLM智能体和人类参与者的实验显示，性能随依赖深度增加显著下降：人类从难度5的98.3%降至难度25的80.0%，最佳模型从90.0%降至60.0%。轨迹分析表明，失败主要源于长期状态跟踪、线索遵循和中间结果传播的断裂。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能够处理局部工具使用，但在深层上下文依赖方面仍存在显著困难。AgentEscapeBench可作为诊断性测试平台，用于衡量当前智能体能力并指导未来的训练工作，朝向更稳健的通用推理、行动和适应能力发展。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external tools, it is important to evaluate their ability to sustain tool-grounded reasoning beyond familiar workflows and short-range interactions. We introduce AgentEscapeBench, an escape-room-style benchmark that tests whether agents can infer, execute, and revise novel tool-use procedures under explicit long-range dependency constraints. Each task defines a directed acyclic dependency graph over tools and items, requiring agents to invoke real external functions, track hidden state revealed incrementally, propagate intermediate results, and submit a deterministically verifiable final answer. AgentEscapeBench includes 270 instances across five difficulty tiers and supports fully automated evaluation. Experiments with sixteen LLM agents and human participants show that performance drops sharply as dependency depth increases: humans decline from 98.3% success at difficulty-5 to 80.0% at difficulty-25, while the best model drops from 90.0% to 60.0%. Trajectory analysis attributes model failures mainly to breakdowns in long-range state tracking, clue adherence, and intermediate-result propagation. These findings suggest that current agents can often handle local tool use but still struggle with deep contextual dependencies. We hope AgentEscapeBench can serve as a diagnostic testbed for measuring current agent capabilities and informing future training efforts toward more robust general-purpose reasoning, action, and adaptation.

</details>


### [256] [TraceFix: Repairing Agent Coordination Protocols with TLA+ Counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07935)
*Shuren Xia,Qiwei Li,Taqiya Ehsan,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TraceFix，一种面向LLM多智能体协调的"验证优先"流水线。智能体从任务描述合成协议拓扑（结构化IR），生成PlusCal协调逻辑，并通过TLA+模型检查器（TLC）的反例迭代修复直至验证通过。验证后的进程体编译为各智能体系统提示，在运行时监控器下执行，拒绝越拓扑协调操作。在48个任务（16个场景族）上全部通过TLC验证，62.5%一次通过，最多4次修复。状态空间跨6个数量级，验证均在60秒内完成。3,456次运行时对比显示拓扑监控执行获得最高任务完成率（平均89.4%，完全81.5%），且当模型能力下降时，使用验证协议的系统退化速度约为纯提示和纯聊天基线的二分之一。消融实验表明，TLC验证协议将死锁/活锁从31.1%降至14.1%，故障注入下差异最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在协调时容易产生死锁、活锁等协议违例问题，缺乏形式化保证。现有方法依赖提示工程或对话式协调，缺乏对协调协议正确性的系统验证机制。

Method: 提出"验证优先"流水线TraceFix：(1) 智能体从任务描述合成协议拓扑作为结构化中间表示；(2) 生成PlusCal协调逻辑；(3) 使用TLA+模型检查器（TLC）反例迭代修复直至验证通过；(4) 验证后的进程体编译为各智能体系统提示；(5) 运行时监控器拒绝越拓扑协调操作。

Result: 48任务全部通过TLC验证，62.5%一次通过，最多4次修复；验证均在60秒内完成；运行时对比中拓扑监控执行获得最高任务完成率（平均89.4%，完全81.5%）；模型能力下降时退化速度约为基线的一半；TLC验证协议将死锁/活锁从31.1%降至14.1%。

Conclusion: 验证优先的流水线能有效生成可证明正确的多智能体协调协议，显著降低死锁/活锁，提升任务完成率，且对模型能力退化具有更好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present TraceFix, a verification-first pipeline for Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent coordination. An agent synthesizes a protocol topology as a structured intermediate representation (IR) from a task description, generates PlusCal coordination logic, and iteratively repairs the protocol using counterexamples from the TLA+ model checker (TLC) until verification succeeds. Verified process bodies are compiled into per-agent system prompts and executed under a runtime monitor that rejects out-of-topology coordination operations. On 48 tasks spanning 16 scenario families, all tasks reach full TLC verification; 62.5% pass on the first attempt and none requires more than four repair iterations. State spaces span six orders of magnitude yet verification completes in under 60 s for every task. A 3,456-run runtime comparison shows that topology-monitored execution achieves the highest task completion (89.4% average, 81.5% full) and that runtimes using the verified protocol degrade at roughly half the rate of prompt-only and chat-only baselines when model capability is reduced. A paired ablation under a fixed runtime shows that TLC-verified protocols cut deadlock/livelock (DL/LL) from 31.1% to 14.1%, with the largest separation under fault injection.

</details>


### [257] [The Limits of AI-Driven Allocation: Optimal Screening under Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07979)
*Santiago Cortes-Gomez,Mateo Dulce Rubio,Carlos Patino,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定预算下，如何最优结合直接筛查（screening）和基于机器学习的算法定向（algorithmic targeting）进行两阶段资源分配。最优策略是在算法分配的边际单位上实施筛查，而直接将资源分配给最高风险单位。筛查与算法定向在效率上呈互补或替代关系：当人群中偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）增大时，筛查带来的效率提升更显著。在哥伦比亚收入保护和社会排雷项目中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习驱动的算法定向虽然更廉价高效，但不可约的偶然不确定性（个体脆弱性状态的随机性）导致资源误分配无法完全消除。作者希望探索如何将传统的直接筛查（获得真实状态）与算法定向（基于预测风险）最优结合，以在固定覆盖预算下最小化资源误分配。

Method: 构建两阶段分配框架：第一阶段（筛查）对部分单位进行直接观察获取真实脆弱性状态；第二阶段（分配）在固定预算下基于预测风险分数进行算法分配。通过理论推导最优筛查策略，并用哥伦比亚收入保障计划和地雷危害清除两个应用案例进行实证分析。

Result: 最优策略是对算法分配边际上的单位进行筛查，而直接分配资源给最高风险单位；筛查与算法定向的效率关系取决于偶然不确定性程度——不确定性越强，筛查效率增益越大；在哥伦比亚的两个应用场景中验证了筛查成本与分配效率之间的权衡具有重要的操作意义。

Conclusion: 算法定向与直接筛查并非互斥，而是可以最优结合。在固定预算下，应优先对算法分配的不确定性较高的边际单位进行筛查，而非对所有单位或随机筛查；筛查的价值随人群中偶然不确定性的增大而上升。

Abstract: The rise of machine learning has shifted targeted resource allocation in policy and humanitarian settings toward algorithmic targeting based on predicted risk scores. This approach is typically cheaper and faster than traditional screening procedures that directly observe the latent vulnerability status through physical verification. Yet, even access to the true conditional vulnerability probability cannot eliminate misallocation: aleatoric uncertainty over individual vulnerability status is irreducible, and probabilistic targeting inevitably misallocates some resources. In this work we study how screening and algorithmic targeting should be optimally combined in a two-stage allocation framework where a screening stage observes true outcomes for a subset of units before a final allocation stage assigns the resource under a fixed coverage budget. We show that the optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation, while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Furthermore, we empirically characterize when screening and algorithmic targeting act as complements or substitutes: efficiency gains from screening grow as the aleatoric uncertainty in the population increases. We illustrate our framework with applications in income-based social protection programs and humanitarian demining in Colombia, where the tension between screening costs and allocation efficiency is operationally consequential.

</details>


### [258] [Abductive Reasoning with Probabilistic Commonsense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08011)
*Joseph Cotnareanu,Chiara Roverato,Han Zhou,Didier Chetelat,Yingxue Zhang,Mark Coates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率溯因常识推理框架PACS，通过LLM和形式化求解器采样不同个体的常识信念并进行聚合，解决常识推理中个体信念差异问题，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号框架中形式化求解器缺乏常识世界知识，而先前方法隐式假设常识事实具有普遍共识，忽略了常识信念在个体间的差异性。

Method: 提出PACS算法，利用LLM和形式化求解器采样证明作为个体不同常识信念的观测，并对这些样本的结论进行聚合，实现概率化的溯因常识推理。

Result: PACS在多个基准上优于链式思维推理、先前神经符号方法和基于搜索的方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模个体间的常识信念差异并进行概率聚合，能够有效提升LLM在溯因常识推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Recent efforts to improve the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have focused on integrating formal logic solvers within neurosymbolic frameworks. A key challenge is that formal solvers lack commonsense world knowledge, preventing them from making reasoning steps that humans find obvious. Prior methods address this by using LLMs to supply missing commonsense assumptions, but these approaches implicitly assume universal agreement on such commonsense facts. In reality, commonsense beliefs vary across individuals. We propose a probabilistic framework for abductive commonsense reasoning that explicitly models this variation, aiming to determine whether most people would judge a statement as true or false. We introduce Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense (PACS), a novel algorithm that uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample proofs as observations of individuals' distinct commonsense beliefs, and aggregates conclusions across these samples. Empirically, PACS outperforms chain-of-thought reasoning, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [259] [Learning CLI Agents with Structured Action Credit under Selective Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08013)
*Haoyang Su,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出σ-Reveal（推理时选择性观察机制）和A³（基于动作优势分配的强化学习方法）来解决CLI智能体在代码库环境中的两个关键瓶颈：部分观测下的证据识别和稀疏奖励下的信用分配，并通过ShellOps数据集进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLI智能体在文件系统交互中面临两大瓶颈：1) 从部分观测的大规模代码库中识别任务相关证据；2) 在长多轮轨迹中将稀疏的终端奖励合理分配给具体动作。现有RL方法未充分利用CLI动作的原生结构化属性作为学习信号。

Method: 提出两个方法：1) σ-Reveal，一种推理时的token预算上下文选择性机制，用于优化观察；2) A³（Action Advantage Assignment），一种原生智能体RL方法，构建回合级相对反馈的回合级优势、基于AST的动作子链残差以及树级轨迹边际。

Result: 构建了ShellOps数据集，包含可验证的CLI任务场景，覆盖仓库环境中的信息提取和文件编辑任务。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化的动作优势分配和选择性观察机制，有效解决了CLI智能体在部分观测和稀疏奖励下的学习瓶颈。

Abstract: Command line interface (CLI) agents are emerging as a practical paradigm for agent-computer interaction over evolving filesystems, executable command line programs, and online execution feedback. Recent work has used reinforcement learning (RL) to learn these interaction abilities from verifiable task feedback, yet few methods exploit the native structured attributes of CLI actions as learning signals. Beyond this underused action structure, CLI learning also couples two bottlenecks for coding agents. First, the agent must identify task-relevant evidence in a large codebase from partial observations. Second, sparse terminal rewards must be assigned to the actions that shape a long multi-turn trajectory. We study these bottlenecks through shell-driven information extraction and file editing tasks. For selective observation, we introduce $σ$-Reveal, an inference-time mechanism that selects token-budgeted context for the same CLI. For credit assignment, we propose Action Advantage Assignment ($\mathrm{A}^3$), a native agentic RL method that preserves the algorithmic complexity of standard agentic RL. $\mathrm{A}^3$ constructs turn-level advantages from episode-level relative feedback, abstract syntax tree (AST) based action sub-chain residuals, and tree-level trajectory margins. To further evaluate this problem setting, we construct ShellOps, a verifiable dataset suite covering CLI tasks in repository environments.

</details>


### [260] [Reason to Play: Behavioral and Brain Alignment Between Frontier LRMs and Human Game Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08019)
*Botos Csaba,Sreejan Kumar,Austin Tudor David Andrews,Laurence Hunt,Chris Summerfield,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Rui Ponte Costa,Marcelo G. Mattar,Momchil Tomov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比大型推理模型（LRMs）、深度强化学习智能体和贝叶斯理论智能体在复杂人类游戏玩法中的表现，发现LRMs最接近人类行为模式，并能以数量级优势预测大脑活动，表明LRMs是复杂自然环境中人类学习和决策的有效计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现代AI系统能否像人类一样在新环境中快速学习抽象知识并灵活运用以指导高效智能的行动。

Method: 使用包含同步fMRI记录的人类复杂游戏玩法数据集，让参与者学习需要规则发现、假设修正和多步规划的新视频游戏。将前沿大型推理模型（LRMs）与无模型、基于模型的深度强化学习智能体以及贝叶斯理论智能体进行比较，评估它们在玩游戏、匹配人类学习行为和预测脑活动方面的能力。

Result: 前沿LRMs最接近人类游戏发现阶段的行为模式，在预测大脑活动方面比两种强化学习替代方案高出约一个数量级，涵盖皮层和皮层下区域，且效应在置换控制检验中稳健。通过目标性操纵进一步表明，大脑对齐反映的是模型对游戏状态的上下文表征，而非其下游规划或推理。

Conclusion: LRMs可作为复杂自然环境中人类学习和决策的有力计算模型。

Abstract: Humans rapidly learn abstract knowledge when encountering novel environments and flexibly deploy this knowledge to guide efficient and intelligent action. Can modern AI systems learn and plan in a similar way? We study this question using a dataset of complex human gameplay with concurrent fMRI recordings, in which participants learn novel video games that require rule discovery, hypothesis revision, and multi-step planning. We jointly evaluate models by their ability to play the games, match human learning behavior, and predict brain activity during the same task, comparing a suite of frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) against model-free and model-based deep reinforcement learning agents and a Bayesian theory-based agent. We find that frontier LRMs most closely match human behavioral patterns during game discovery and predict brain activity an order of magnitude better than both reinforcement learning alternatives across cortical and subcortical regions, with effects robust to permutation controls. Through targeted manipulations, we further show that brain alignment reflects the model's in-context representation of the game state rather than its downstream planning or reasoning. Our results establish LRMs as compelling computational accounts of human learning and decision making in complex, naturalistic environments. Project page with interactive replays: https://botcs.github.io/reason-to-play/

</details>


### [261] [VecCISC: Improving Confidence-Informed Self-Consistency with Reasoning Trace Clustering and Candidate Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08070)
*James Petullo,Sonny George,Dylan Cashman,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VecCISC是一种轻量级自适应框架，通过语义相似性过滤推理迹，在保持或提升加权多数投票准确率的同时，将总token使用量减少47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权多数投票（如CISC）相比标准自一致性方法更准确，但需要调用批评者LLM为每个候选答案生成置信度分数，导致巨大的开销和成本。为降低这一开销，作者提出VecCISC。

Method: VecCISC使用语义相似性度量过滤与已有答案语义等价、退化或幻觉的推理迹，从而减少需要由批评者LLM评估的候选答案数量。

Result: 在数学、化学、生物学、常识推理和人文学科五个领域的基准测试中，VecCISC将总token使用量降低47%，同时保持或超过CISC的准确率。

Conclusion: VecCISC通过语义过滤有效降低了加权多数投票的计算成本，且不牺牲准确率，是一种实用的推理时扩展技术。

Abstract: A standard technique for scaling inference-time reasoning is Self-Consistency, whereby multiple candidate answers are sampled from an LLM and the most common answer is selected. More recently, it has been shown that weighted majority voting (e.g. Confidence-Informed Self Consistency (CISC)), which assigns a confidence value to each candidate answer and chooses the answer with the largest accumulated score, tends to be more accurate on a wide range of popular benchmarks. In practice, weighted majority voting necessitates calling a critic LLM on each candidate's reasoning trace to produce the answer's confidence score. This secondary series of LLM calls greatly increases the overhead and cost of weighted majority voting, despite its potential performance benefits. To reduce this expense, we propose VecCISC, a lightweight, adaptive framework that uses a measure of semantic similarity to filter reasoning traces that are semantically equivalent to others, degenerate, or hallucinated, thus decreasing the number of candidate answers that must be evaluated by the critic. To ensure adequate experimental thoroughness, we evaluate VecCISC on five challenging, widely-adopted datasets spanning the domains of mathematics, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and the humanities. Our results demonstrate that VecCISC reduces the total token usage by 47%, while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of CISC.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Velocity-Space 3D Asset Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07385)
*Hao Liu,Yuxuan Lin,Jingfeng Guo,Ruihang Chu,Junjie Wang,Ruotong Li,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: VS3D 提出了一种基于速度空间、无需反演/训练/掩码的3D局部编辑框架，通过在ODE采样器内部进行三项针对性干预，解决现有方法中身份泄露、编辑信号放大不足和全局条件拖拽三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D局部编辑方法依赖外部机制（如3D掩码、体素合并、2D多视角提升），未能在问题根源——ODE采样器内部进行干预，导致整流流生成器无法同时满足编辑区域强速度场与保留区域零速度场的矛盾需求，产生身份泄露、缺乏专用编辑放大通道、全局条件拖拽三个问题。

Method: VS3D包含三个互补模块：(1) RASI - 通过源重建校准将无条件嵌入转化为逐步资产锚点，吸收身份泄露；(2) PMG - 对比高/低质量子样本估计的速度差，仅在存在一致编辑的区域激活，放大编辑信号；(3) TAR - 让采样器在几何和材质阶段逐token决定保留内容。

Result: VS3D实现了无需反演、无需训练、无需掩码的3D局部编辑，有效解决了身份泄露、编辑信号放大的权衡问题以及全局条件拖拽。

Conclusion: VS3D通过在ODE采样器内部进行结构化干预，为整流流生成器提供了忠实且局部的3D编辑能力，是首个从速度场层面系统性解决局部编辑的方法。

Abstract: Editing a 3D asset locally, modifying a target region while preserving the rest, is a fundamental requirement of native 3D editing. Existing methods enforce locality through mechanisms external to the generator, such as manual 3D masks, post-hoc voxel merging, or 2D multi-view lifting. None of them intervene where the corruption actually originates: inside the ODE sampler. For a rectified-flow generator to achieve faithful local editing, its velocity field should be strong over the target editing region while vanishing on preserved content. Yet a single velocity field can hardly satisfy both requirements simultaneously, leading to three problems: (i) identity leakage that keeps the edit signal non-zero on preserved regions; (ii) no dedicated edit-amplification channel, so strengthening the edit inevitably perturbs identity; and (iii) an identity drag at the geometry and material stages, where a global condition pulls every token toward the target. We propose VS3D (Velocity-Space 3D Asset editing}), an inversion-free, training-free, and mask-free framework that addresses each problem with a targeted intervention inside the sampler. VS3D integrates three complementary modules, each corresponding to a specific stage of the editing pipeline. Reconstruction-Anchored Source Injection (RASI) absorbs identity leakage by turning the unconditional embedding into a per-step, asset-specific anchor calibrated through source reconstruction. Partial-Mean Guidance (PMG) amplifies the edit signal by contrasting high- and low-quality subsample estimates of the velocity difference, active only where a consistent edit exists. Twin-Agreement Residual injection (TAR) lets the sampler decide token by token what to preserve at the geometry and material stages.

</details>


### [263] [LoBoFit: Flexible Garment Refitting via Local Bone Mapping Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07450)
*Meng Zhang,Yu Xin,Feiya Guo,Kaizhang Kang,Mengyu Chu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出LoBoFit，一种基于局部骨骼映射混合（LoBoMap Blending）表示的服装重拟合方法。该方法将服装几何表示为局部骨骼坐标帧中映射的线性混合，从而解耦全局坐标的复杂耦合，实现鲁棒、高效且保细节的服装跨体型/姿态迁移，在鲁棒性和输出质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服装重拟合方法在平衡鲁棒性、效率和细节保真度方面存在困难：物理仿真成本高、数据驱动方法泛化性差、全局顶点空间几何优化病态且易陷入局部最优。根本原因在于直接在全局坐标系中变形服装会导致顶点非局部耦合，形成复杂且结构不良的优化景观。

Method: 提出LoBoMap Blending表示，将服装几何表达为各顶点在局部骨骼坐标帧中映射的线性混合；利用局部骨骼映射提供姿态鲁棒的初始化和良态参数化，混合权重平滑优化景观并扩大可行解空间；后续通过优化局部残差高效解决碰撞并保持细节，将全局变形分解为可管理的子问题。

Result: 在具有大形状和拓扑差异的虚拟人之间，可靠地重拟合高分辨率单层和多层服装，忠实保留复杂褶皱和预期合身风格，在鲁棒性和输出质量上超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 全局坐标系的直接变形是服装重拟合问题的根本瓶颈，基于局部骨骼映射混合的表示方法能有效解耦全局耦合，同时保证表达力、灵活性和优化稳定性，为服装重拟合提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Garment refitting, the task of adapting a garment from a source to a target avatar, must preserve the original design features and fine-scale wrinkles, a challenge exacerbated by significant shape variations and varying poses without registration to a shared canonical pose. Existing methods struggle to balance robustness, efficiency, and fidelity of detail: physics-based simulation is costly, data-driven approaches lack generalizability, and geometry optimization in the full vertex space is often ill-conditioned and prone to local minima with unsatisfactory quality. We identify that a fundamental limitation lies in the representation: deforming garments directly in global coordinates couples vertices non-locally, creating a complex and poorly-structured optimization landscape. Therefore, we introduce LoBoFit, a robust refitting method built upon a novel Local Bone Mapping Blending (LoBoMap Blending) representation. Instead of manipulating global vertex positions, LoBoMap Blending expresses garment geometry as a linear blend of its mappings into local bone coordinate frames. This representation is highly expressive and flexible: local bone mappings yield a pose-robust initialization and a well-conditioned parameterization, while blending weights smooth the optimization landscape and broaden the space of plausible solutions for stable convergence with fine-scale detail preservation. The subsequent refinement efficiently resolves collisions and preserves details by optimizing localized residuals, effectively decomposing the complex global deformation into manageable subproblems. Our experiments demonstrate that LoBoFit reliably refits high-resolution, single- and multi-layer garments across avatars with large shape and topological differences, while faithfully preserving intricate wrinkles and the intended fit style, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in robustness and output quality.

</details>
